as ChatOrchestrator;\n  const session: ChatSession = {sessionId: 's1', userId: 'u1', entries: []};\n\n  const handle = createMessageHandler(orchestrator, sessionStore);\n\n  return {\n    session,\n    processMessageStream,\n    sent,\n    frames: () => wire(sent),\n    send: (raw: unknown = {type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'}, signal?: AbortSignal) =>\n      handle(ws, session, raw, signal),\n  };\n};","originalFile":"import {jest} from '@jest/globals';\nimport {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport {createMessageHandler} from './messageHandler.js';\nimport {createChatOrchestrator, TOOL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport {ToolRegistry} from '../../orchestration/tools/registry.js';\nimport {PromptBuilder} from '../../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\nimport type {AIProvider, MessageChunk, ToolCall} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport {AIError} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport type {ChatSession, SessionEntry} from '../../types/session.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../../types/messages.js';\nimport {PerSessionLock} from '../../shared/perSessionLock.js';\nimport {deferred} from '../wsTestSupport.js';\n\n// The frames, in order, are the contract - above all for suppression, which retracts.\n\n// One line per frame; entries carry generated timestamps no assertion should read.\nconst wire = (sent: OutgoingMessage[]): string[] =>\n  sent.map(frame => {\n    if (frame.type === 'entry_upsert') {\n      const content = 'content' in frame.entry ? frame.entry.content : '';\n      return `upsert ${frame.index} ${frame.entry.role} ${JSON.stringify(content)}`;\n    }\n    if (frame.type === 'entry_delta') return `delta ${frame.index} ${JSON.stringify(frame.delta)}`;\n    if (frame.type === 'entry_commit') return `commit ${frame.index}`;\n    if (frame.type === 'entry_skip') return `skip ${frame.index}`;\n    if (frame.type === 'error') return `error ${frame.code}`;\n    if (frame.type === 'pong') return `pong ${frame.timestamp}`;\n    return frame.type;\n  });\n\nconst text = (value: string): MessageChunk => ({type: 'text', text: value});\nconst STOP: MessageChunk = {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\nconst SUPPRESS = {type: 'suppress_output'} as unknown as MessageChunk;\n\ninterface StreamScript {\n  failWith?: Error;\n  pauseAfter?: {chunks: number; until: Promise<void>};\n}\n\nconst harness = (chunks: MessageChunk[], {failWith, pauseAfter}: StreamScript = {}) => {\n  const sent: OutgoingMessage[] = [];\n  const ws = {send: (raw: string) => sent.push(JSON.parse(raw) as OutgoingMessage)} as unknown as WebSocket;\n\n  const stream = async function* (): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n    for (const [position, chunk] of chunks.entries()) {\n      if (pauseAfter && position === pauseAfter.chunks) await pauseAfter.until;\n      yield chunk;\n    }\n    if (failWith) throw failWith;\n  };\n\n  const processMessageStream = jest.fn(stream);\n  const orchestrator = {processMessageStream} as unknown as ChatOrchestrator;\n  const session: ChatSession = {sessionId: 's1', userId: 'u1', entries: []};\n\n  const handle = createMessageHandler(orchestrator);\n\n  return {\n    session,\n    processMessageStream,\n    sent,\n    frames: () => wire(sent),\n    send: (raw: unknown = {type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'}, signal?: AbortSignal) =>\n      handle(ws, session, raw, signal),\n  };\n};\n\ndescribe('the websocket message handler', () => {\n  describe('dispatch', () => {\n    it('handles list_sessions with a sessionStore by sending session_list', async () => {\n      const sessions = [\n        {\n          sessionId: 's1',\n          createdAt: new Date('2026-01-01'),\n          updatedAt: new Date('2026-01-02'),\n          entryCount: 2,\n        },\n      ];\n      const sessionStore = {\n        listFor: jest.fn(async () => sessions),\n      } as unknown as import('../../types/sessionStore.js').SessionStore;\n      const h = harness([], {}, sessionStore);\n\n      await h.send({type: 'list_sessions', limit: 10});\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toContain('session_list');\n    });\n\n    it('rejects an unparseable message and never starts a stream', async () => {\n      const h = harness([]);\n\n      await h.send({type: 'nonsense'});\n\n      const {calls} = h.processMessageStream.mock;\n\n      expect({frames: h.frames(), streams: calls.length}).toEqual({frames: ['error INVALID_MESSAGE'], streams: 0});\n    });\n\n    it('answers a ping with the timestamp it was given', async () => {\n      const h = harness([]);\n\n      await h.send({type: 'ping', timestamp: 1234});\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual(['pong 1234']);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('a plain assistant reply', () => {\n    it('sends the user entry, then the assistant entry, its delta, its commit and stream_end', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('hi there'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual([\n        'upsert 0 user \"hello\"',\n        'upsert 1 assistant \"\"',\n        'delta 1 \"hi there\"',\n        'commit 1',\n        'stream_end',\n      ]);\n    });\n\n    it('leaves the committed assistant entry in the session, no longer streaming', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('hi there'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const {entries} = h.session;\n\n      expect(entries[1]).toMatchObject({role: 'assistant', content: 'hi there', isStreaming: false});\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('thinking segments', () => {\n    it('commits the thinking entry before the assistant entry opens', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('<thinking>pondering</thinking>answer'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual([\n        'upsert 0 user \"hello\"',\n        'upsert 1 thinking \"\"',\n        'delta 1 \"pondering\"',\n        'commit 1',\n        'upsert 2 assistant \"\"',\n        'delta 2 \"answer\"',\n        'commit 2',\n        'stream_end',\n      ]);\n    });\n\n    it('commits the thinking entry when text resumes, not when a tool entry appears', async () => {\n      const h = harness([\n        text('<thinking>I should look up the weather for Berlin.</thinking>'),\n        {type: 'tool_use', toolCall: {id: 'c1', name: 'get_weather', input: {location: 'Berlin'}}} as MessageChunk,\n        text('Berlin currently has '),\n        text('a temperature of 18 degrees Celsius with clear skies.'),\n        STOP,\n      ]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual([\n        'upsert 0 user \"hello\"',\n        'upsert 1 thinking \"\"',\n        'delta 1 \"I should look up the weather for Berlin.\"',\n        'upsert 2 tool \"\"',\n        'commit 1',\n        'upsert 3 assistant \"\"',\n        'delta 3 \"Berlin currently has \"',\n        'delta 3 \"a temperature of 18 degrees Celsius with clear skies.\"',\n        'commit 3',\n        'stream_end',\n      ]);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('tool chunks', () => {\n    it('sends a tool entry for a tool call', async () => {\n      const h = harness([{type: 'tool_use', toolCall: {id: 'c1', name: 'lookup', input: {q: 1}}}, STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual(['upsert 0 user \"hello\"', 'upsert 1 tool \"\"', 'stream_end']);\n    });\n\n    it('forwards a tool result to the client untouched', async () => {\n      const forwarded = {type: 'entry_commit', index: 41} as OutgoingMessage;\n      const h = harness([{type: 'tool_result', clientMessages: [forwarded]} as MessageChunk, STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual(['upsert 0 user \"hello\"', 'commit 41', 'stream_end']);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('suppression', () => {\n    it('blanks an assistant entry that was already sent', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('leaked'), STOP, SUPPRESS]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual([\n        'upsert 0 user \"hello\"',\n        'upsert 1 assistant \"\"',\n        'delta 1 \"leaked\"',\n        'commit 1',\n        'upsert 1 assistant \"\"',\n        'stream_end',\n      ]);\n    });\n\n    it('never blanks a thinking entry, only assistant ones', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('<thinking>private</thinking>said'), STOP, SUPPRESS]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames().filter(frame => frame.startsWith('upsert 1'))).toEqual(['upsert 1 thinking \"\"']);\n    });\n\n    it('skips rather than upserts a segment opened after suppression', async () => {\n      const h = harness([SUPPRESS, text('hidden'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual(['upsert 0 user \"hello\"', 'skip 1', 'stream_end']);\n    });\n\n    it('still records a suppressed reply in the session, so history keeps it', async () => {\n      const h = harness([SUPPRESS, text('hidden'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const {entries} = h.session;\n\n      expect(entries[1]).toMatchObject({role: 'assistant', content: 'hidden', isStreaming: false});\n    });\n\n    it('does not resurrect an entry the client only ever saw skipped', async () => {\n      const h = harness([SUPPRESS, text('hidden'), STOP, SUPPRESS]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual(['upsert 0 user \"hello\"', 'skip 1', 'stream_end']);\n    });\n\n    it('blanks each sent entry once, so a second suppression repeats nothing', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('leaked'), STOP, SUPPRESS, SUPPRESS]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames().filter(frame => frame === 'upsert 1 assistant \"\"')).toHaveLength(2);\n    });\n\n    it('marks a sent assistant entry it blanked as suppressed in the session, keeping its text', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('leaked'), STOP, SUPPRESS]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const [, answer] = h.session.entries;\n      expect(answer).toMatchObject({role: 'assistant', content: 'leaked', suppressed: true});\n    });\n\n    it('marks a segment opened after suppression as suppressed in the session', async () => {\n      const h = harness([SUPPRESS, text('hidden'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const [, answer] = h.session.entries;\n      expect(answer).toMatchObject({role: 'assistant', content: 'hidden', suppressed: true});\n    });\n\n    it('leaves a thinking entry the client kept seeing unmarked, and marks the blanked answer', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('<thinking>private</thinking>said'), STOP, SUPPRESS]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const [, thinking, answer] = h.session.entries;\n      expect({thinking: 'suppressed' in thinking, answer}).toMatchObject({\n        thinking: false,\n        answer: {role: 'assistant', content: 'said', suppressed: true},\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('commits and retracts an answer still open when suppression begins, and skips what follows as a new entry', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('before'), SUPPRESS, text(' after'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const [, answer, after] = h.session.entries;\n      expect({frames: h.frames(), answer, after}).toMatchObject({\n        frames: [\n          'upsert 0 user \"hello\"',\n          'upsert 1 assistant \"\"',\n          'delta 1 \"before\"',\n          'commit 1',\n          'upsert 1 assistant \"\"',\n          'skip 2',\n          'stream_end',\n        ],\n        answer: {role: 'assistant', content: 'before', isStreaming: false, suppressed: true},\n        after: {role: 'assistant', content: ' after', suppressed: true},\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('commits a thought still open when suppression begins as the client saw it, and skips what follows', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('<thinking>early'), SUPPRESS, text(' later</thinking>'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const [, thought, after] = h.session.entries;\n      expect({frames: h.frames(), thought, marked: 'suppressed' in thought, after}).toMatchObject({\n        frames: [\n          'upsert 0 user \"hello\"',\n          'upsert 1 thinking \"\"',\n          'delta 1 \"early\"',\n          'commit 1',\n          'skip 2',\n          'stream_end',\n        ],\n        thought: {role: 'thinking', content: 'early', isStreaming: false},\n        marked: false,\n        after: {role: 'thinking', content: ' later', suppressed: true},\n      });\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('failures', () => {\n    it('tells the client to retry when the provider is rate limited', async () => {\n      // eslint-disable-next-line re-lint/no-flag-params -- AIError's retryable flag; see adrs/ADR-006-lint-suppressions.md\n      const h = harness([], {failWith: new AIError('slow down', 'RATE_LIMITED', true)});\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual(['upsert 0 user \"hello\"', 'error RATE_LIMITED', 'stream_end']);\n    });\n\n    it('reports any other failure as a server error', async () => {\n      const h = harness([], {failWith: new Error('boom')});\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual(['upsert 0 user \"hello\"', 'error SERVER_ERROR', 'stream_end']);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('the terminal frame', () => {\n    it('ends a provider failure mid-stream with error then stream_end, stream_end last', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('Half an ans')], {failWith: new Error('provider dropped')});\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual([\n        'upsert 0 user \"hello\"',\n        'upsert 1 assistant \"\"',\n        'delta 1 \"Half an ans\"',\n        'upsert 1 assistant \"Half an ans\"',\n        'commit 1',\n        'error SERVER_ERROR',\n        'stream_end',\n      ]);\n    });\n\n    const invalidRun = async (content: string) => {\n      const h = harness([]);\n      await h.send({type: 'user_message', content});\n      const {calls} = h.processMessageStream.mock;\n      return {frames: h.frames(), streams: calls.length};\n    };\n    const REFUSED = {frames: ['error INVALID_MESSAGE', 'stream_end'], streams: 0};\n\n    it('answers an empty user_message with INVALID_MESSAGE then stream_end', async () => {\n      expect(await invalidRun('')).toEqual(REFUSED);\n    });\n\n    it('answers a blank user_message with INVALID_MESSAGE then stream_end', async () => {\n      expect(await invalidRun('   ')).toEqual(REFUSED);\n    });\n\n    it('answers an over-length user_message with INVALID_MESSAGE then stream_end', async () => {\n      expect(await invalidRun('x'.repeat(10_001))).toEqual(REFUSED);\n    });\n\n    it('answers a malformed ping with INVALID_MESSAGE and no stream_end', async () => {\n      const h = harness([]);\n\n      await h.send({type: 'ping', timestamp: 'soon'});\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual(['error INVALID_MESSAGE']);\n    });\n\n    it('keeps one stream_end, last, when a malformed ping lands mid-run', async () => {\n      const gate = deferred();\n      const h = harness([text('first '), text('second'), STOP], {pauseAfter: {chunks: 1, until: gate.until}});\n\n      const run = h.send();\n      await new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));\n      await h.send({type: 'ping', timestamp: 'soon'});\n      gate.release();\n      await run;\n\n      expect(h.frames()).toEqual([\n        'upsert 0 user \"hello\"',\n        'upsert 1 assistant \"\"',\n        'delta 1 \"first \"',\n        'error INVALID_MESSAGE',\n        'delta 1 \"second\"',\n        'commit 1',\n        'stream_end',\n      ]);\n    });\n\n    it('sends one stream_end for a three-round tool conversation', async () => {\n      const call = (id: string): MessageChunk => ({type: 'tool_use', toolCall: {id, name: 'lookup', input: {id}}});\n      const h = harness([call('c1'), call('c2'), call('c3'), text('Done'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const frames = h.frames();\n      expect({ends: frames.filter(frame => frame === 'stream_end').length, last: frames.at(-1)}).toEqual({\n        ends: 1,\n        last: 'stream_end',\n      });\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('open entries on every terminal path', () => {\n    const stillStreaming = ({entries}: ChatSession): number =>\n      entries.filter(entry => 'isStreaming' in entry && entry.isStreaming).length;\n    const DROPPED = new Error('provider dropped');\n\n    it('commits a partial answer as streamed, before the error, when the provider throws', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('partial answer')], {failWith: DROPPED});\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const [, answer] = h.session.entries;\n      expect({frames: h.frames(), open: stillStreaming(h.session), answer}).toMatchObject({\n        frames: [\n          'upsert 0 user \"hello\"',\n          'upsert 1 assistant \"\"',\n          'delta 1 \"partial answer\"',\n          'upsert 1 assistant \"partial answer\"',\n          'commit 1',\n          'error SERVER_ERROR',\n          'stream_end',\n        ],\n        open: 0,\n        answer: {role: 'assistant', content: 'partial answer', isStreaming: false},\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('commits an open thinking entry too when the provider throws mid-thought', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('<thinking>half a thou')], {failWith: DROPPED});\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect({\n        commits: h.frames().filter(frame => frame.startsWith('commit')),\n        open: stillStreaming(h.session),\n      }).toEqual({\n        commits: ['commit 1'],\n        open: 0,\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('commits the answer, flagged truncated, when a round ends on a tool call with no stop chunk', async () => {\n      const lookup: MessageChunk = {type: 'tool_use', toolCall: {id: 'c1', name: 'lookup', input: {}}};\n      const h = harness([text('Let me check'), lookup]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const [, answer] = h.session.entries;\n      expect({last: h.frames().slice(-2), open: stillStreaming(h.session), answer}).toMatchObject({\n        last: ['commit 1', 'stream_end'],\n        open: 0,\n        answer: {role: 'assistant', content: 'Let me check', truncated: true},\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('sends one commit per opened entry when the turn ends on a stop chunk', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('<thinking>pondering</thinking>answer'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect(h.frames().filter(frame => frame.startsWith('commit'))).toEqual(['commit 1', 'commit 2']);\n    });\n\n    it('records the open entries under suppression without sending a commit when the provider throws', async () => {\n      const h = harness([SUPPRESS, text('hidden')], {failWith: DROPPED});\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      expect({frames: h.frames(), open: stillStreaming(h.session)}).toEqual({\n        frames: ['upsert 0 user \"hello\"', 'skip 1', 'error SERVER_ERROR', 'stream_end'],\n        open: 0,\n      });\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('an entry cut short', () => {\n    const DROPPED = new Error('provider dropped');\n    const upsertsOf = (sent: OutgoingMessage[], index: number) =>\n      sent.flatMap(frame => (frame.type === 'entry_upsert' && frame.index === index ? [frame.entry] : []));\n\n    it('flags a partial answer truncated in the session and re-sends it with the flag before its commit', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('partial answer')], {failWith: DROPPED});\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const [, stored] = h.session.entries;\n      const resent = upsertsOf(h.sent, 1).at(-1);\n      const frames = h.frames();\n      expect({\n        stored,\n        resent,\n        resentBeforeCommit: frames.lastIndexOf('upsert 1 assistant \"partial answer\"') < frames.indexOf('commit 1'),\n      }).toMatchObject({\n        stored: {role: 'assistant', content: 'partial answer', isStreaming: false, truncated: true},\n        resent: {role: 'assistant', content: 'partial answer', truncated: true},\n        resentBeforeCommit: true,\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('flags an open thinking entry truncated when the provider throws mid-thought', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('<thinking>half a thou')], {failWith: DROPPED});\n\n committed on a stop chunk without the flag and sends it once', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('All done.'), STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const [, answer] = h.session.entries;\n      expect({flagged: 'truncated' in answer, upserts: upsertsOf(h.sent, 1).length}).toEqual({\n        flagged: false,\n        upserts: 1,\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('flags a suppressed entry that a throw cut short in the session but never re-sends it', async () => {\n      const h = harness([SUPPRESS, text('hidden')], {failWith: DROPPED});\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const [, hidden] = h.session.entries;\n      expect({hidden, upserts: upsertsOf(h.sent, 1).length}).toMatchObject({\n        hidden: {content: 'hidden', truncated: true},\n        upserts: 0,\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('keeps the text the thinking-tag parser still held when a throw cuts the answer short', async () => {\n      const h = harness([text('Half <thi')], {failWith: DROPPED});\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const [, stored] = h.session.entries;\n      expect({stored, resent: upsertsOf(h.sent, 1).at(-1)}).toMatchObject({\n        stored: {content: 'Half <thi', truncated: true},\n = harness([SUPPRESS, text('Half <thi')], {failWith: DROPPED});\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const [, hidden] = h.session.entries;\n      expect({hidden, toClient: h.frames().filter(frame => const TOOL_STOP: MessageChunk = {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'tool_use'};\n    const BUDGET_STOP: MessageChunk = {type: 'stop', stopReason: TOOL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED};\n\n    it(\"renders the hook's async () => {\n      const h = harness([LOOKUP, TOOL_STOP, SUPPRESS, text(SENTENCE), BUDGET_STOP]);\n\n      await h.send();\n\n      const frames = h.frames();\n      const [, , stored] = true, deltas: [], stored: {role: 'assistant', content: SENTENCE}});\n    });\n\n    it('sends no frame for the synthetic stop when no hook wrote a sentence, so the run ends as it did without one', withoutIt.send();\n\n      const frames = exhausted.frames();\n      expect({frames, last: frames.at(-1)}).toEqual({frames: withoutIt.frames(), last: 'stream_end'});\n    });\n  });\n});\n\nconst waits at its gate, so a test decides when it ends.\nconst gated = (runs: number) => {\n  const sent: OutgoingMessage[] = [];\n  const socket = {\n    readyState: WebSocket.OPEN as number,\n    send: SessionEntry[][] = [];\n  const processMessageStream = async function* (session: ChatSession): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n    const run = handed.length;\n    handed.push(structuredClone(session.entries));\n    yield text(`answer ${run}`);\n    await gates[run].until;\n    yield STOP;\n  };\n  const session: ChatSession = {sessionId: 's1', userId: 'u1', entries: []};\n  const handle = createMessageHandler({processMessageStream} as unknown as ChatOrchestrator);\n\n  return {\n    session,\n    handed,\n    release: (run: number) => gates[run].release(),\n    close: () => {\n      socket.readyState = WebSocket.CLOSED;\n    },\n    frames: () => wire(sent),\n    send: (raw: unknown) => handle(ws, session, raw),\n  };\n};\n\ndescribe('one message at a time per session', () => {\n  it('answers a second user_message only after the first has sent its stream_end, in the order they arrived', async () => {\n    const await second;\n\n    const {entries} = h.session;\n    expect({\n      entries: entries.map(entry => `${entry.role} ${'content' in entry ? entry.content : ''}`),\n      frames: h.frames(),\n => answerFrame(ws, session, validation, sessionStore));\n  };\n}","originalFile":"import {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport type {SessionEntry} from '../../types/session.js';\nimport type {ChatSession} from '../../types/session.js';\nimport {ErrorCodes} from '../../types/messages.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../../types/messages.js';\nimport {ThinkingTagParser, ParsedSegment} from '../../infrastructure/parsers/thinkingTagParser.js';\nimport {validateMessage} from './validation.js';\nimport type {ValidationResult} from './validation.js';\nimport {PerSessionLock} from '../../shared/perSessionLock.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport {AIError} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport type {MessageChunk} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport {\n  createUserEntry,\n  createAssistantEntry,\n  createThinkingEntry,\n  createToolEntry,\n} from '../../orchestration/entryFactories.js';\nimport {appendEntry, appendDelta, commitEntry, markTruncated} from '../../orchestration/entryMutations.js';\nimport {sendJson, sendUpsert, sendDelta, sendCommit, sendSkip, sendError, sendStreamEnd} from './sender.js';\nimport {log} from '../../shared/logger.js';\n\ninterface StreamState {\n  thinkingIndex: number | null;\n  assistantIndex: number | null;\n  suppressOutput: boolean;\n  sentAssistantIndices: number[];\n}\n\ntype StateIndexKey = 'thinkingIndex' | 'assistantIndex';\ntype EntryFactory = typeof createThinkingEntry | typeof createAssistantEntry;\nexport function createMessageHandler(orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator) {\n  // Keyed by session, not socket: a resumed session can be open on more than one (specs/hal-engine-architecture/spec.md).\n  const lock = new PerSessionLock();\n  return function handleMessage(\n    ws: WebSocket,\n    session: ChatSession,\n    rawMessage: unknown,\n    signal?: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const validation = validateMessage(rawMessage);\n    // Queued with no await before it, so a session's user_messages, a failed one too, are answered in arrival order.\n    if (isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage)) {\n      return lock.run(session.sessionId, () => answerInTurn(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n    }\n    // An async action runs up to its first await at once, so a ping is answered now and a throw stays a rejection.\n    return wsErrorHandler(ws, async () => answerFrame(ws, validation));\n  };\n}\n\n// The whole answer, an error and the stream_end included, goes out inside the lock: an error frame names no message.\nasync function answerInTurn(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  // Its socket closed while it waited: nobody is left to read the answer, and a session's last close took its credentials.\n  if (signal?.aborted || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSING || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSED) {\n    log.info('message', 'queued message dropped: its socket closed', {sessionId: session.sessionId});\n    return;\n  }\n  const startedAt = Date.now();\n  try {\n    await wsErrorHandler(ws, () => answerUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n  } finally {\n    endTurn(ws, session, signal, startedAt);\n  }\n}\n\n// A turn whose socket closed is abandoned rather than ended: nobody is left to read a terminal frame.\nfunction endTurn(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, signal: AbortSignal | undefined, startedAt: number): void {\n  if (signal?.aborted) {\n    log.info('message', 'turn abandoned: its socket closed', {\n      sessionId: session.sessionId,\n      elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n    });\n    return;\n  }\n  sendStreamEnd(ws);\n}\n\nasync function answerUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  const message = validation.valid ? validation.data : undefined;\n  if (message?.type === 'user_message') return handleUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, message.content, signal);\n  answerFrame(ws, validation);\n}\n\nfunction answerFrame(ws: WebSocket, validation: ValidationResult): void {\n  if (!validation.valid) {\n    log.warn('message', 'validation failed', {error: validation.error});\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.INVALID_MESSAGE, validation.error);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const message = validation.data;\n  if (message.type === 'ping') handlePing(ws, message.timestamp);\n}\n\n// Read from the raw frame, so a user_message that fails validation still gets its stream_end.\nfunction isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage: unknown): boolean {\n  return typeof rawMessage === 'object' && rawMessage !== null && Reflect.get(rawMessage, 'type') === 'user_message';\n}\n\nasync function wsErrorHandler(ws: WebSocket, action: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await action();\n  } catch (error) {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';\n    const code = error instanceof AIError ? error.code : 'UNKNOWN';\n    log.error('message', 'handling error', {error: message, code});\n    if (error instanceof AIError && error.code === 'RATE_LIMITED') {\n      sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.RATE_LIMITED, 'AI service is busy, please try again');\n      return;\n    }\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.SERVER_ERROR, 'Failed to process message');\n  }\n}\n\nasync function handleUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  content: string,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  log.info('message', 'user message received', {sessionId: session.sessionId, contentLength: content.length});\n\n  const userEntry = createUserEntry(content);\n  const userIndex = appendEntry(session, userEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, userIndex, userEntry);\n\n  const parser = new ThinkingTagParser();\n  const state: StreamState = {\n    thinkingIndex: null,\n    assistantIndex: null,\n    suppressOutput: false,\n    sentAssistantIndices: [],\n  };\n\n  let textChunkCount = 0;\n\n  try {\n    for await (const chunk of orchestrator.processMessageStream(session, {signal})) {\n      // Its socket closed: breaking returns the run's generator, so the run stops and its own teardown still runs.\n      if (signal?.aborted) break;\n      textChunkCount = logChunk(chunk, textChunkCount);\n      processChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk);\n    }\n  } finally {\n    // Anything still open here was cut short: a stop has already flushed the parser and committed every entry it opened.\n    flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n  }\n\n  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n  }\n  log.info('stream', 'stream completed', {suppressed: state.suppressOutput, entries: session.entries.length});\n}\n\n// Diagnostics only: counts a run of text chunks so it is logged once, not per token.\nfunction logChunk(chunk: MessageChunk, textChunkCount: number): number {\n  if (chunk.type === 'text') return textChunkCount + 1;\n\n  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n  }\n\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') {\n    log.debug('stream', 'tool_result chunk', {\n      messageCount: chunk.clientMessages.length,\n      types: chunk.clientMessages.map(m => m.type),\n    });\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  log.debug('stream', 'chunk', {type: chunk.type});\n  return 0;\n}\n\nfunction processChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n  chunk: MessageChunk\n): void {\n  if (chunk.type === 'text') return processTextChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk.text);\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_use') return processToolUseChunk(ws, session, chunk.toolCall);\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') return processToolResultChunk(ws, chunk.clientMessages);\n  if (chunk.type === 'suppress_output') return processSuppressChunk(ws, session, state);\n  if (chunk.type === 'stop') return processStopChunk(ws, session, state, parser);\n}\n\nfunction processTextChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n  text: string\n): void {\n  const segments = parser.push(text);\n  for (const segment of segments) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction handleTextSegment(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, segment: ParsedSegment): void {\n  if (segment.type === 'thinking') {\n    streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex', createThinkingEntry, segment.content);\n    return;\n  }\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex', createAssistantEntry, segment.content);\n}\n\nfunction streamSegment(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  key: StateIndexKey,\n  createEntry: EntryFactory,\n  content: string\n): void {\n  const isNew = initSegment(session, state, key, createEntry);\n  if (isNew && state.suppressOutput) skipSuppressed(ws, session, state[key]!);\n  if (isNew && !state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, state[key]!, session.entries[state[key]!]);\n  appendDelta(session, state[key]!, content);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendDelta(ws, state[key]!, content);\n}\n\n// Stored as suppressed, so a replay on resume shows the client what it saw live and not the text a tool hid.\nfunction skipSuppressed(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, index: number): void {\n  markSuppressed(session, index);\n  sendSkip(ws, index);\n}\n\nfunction markSuppressed(session: ChatSession, index: number): void {\n  const entry = session.entries[index];\n  if (entry.role === 'assistant' || entry.role === 'thinking') entry.suppressed = true;\n}\n\nfunction initSegment(session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey, createEntry: EntryFactory): boolean {\n  if (state[key] !== null) return false;\n  state[key] = appendEntry(session, createEntry());\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction commitAndClear(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n\n  commitEntry(session, index);\n  state[key] = null;\n\n  // Only what the client received can be retracted, and only assistant entries.\n  if (state.suppressOutput) return;\n\n  sendCommit(ws, index);\n  if (key === 'assistantIndex') state.sentAssistantIndices.push(index);\n}\n\nfunction processToolUseChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  toolCall: {name: string; input: Record<string, unknown>}\n): void {\n  const toolEntry = createToolEntry(toolCall.name, toolCall.input);\n  const toolIndex = appendEntry(session, toolEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, toolIndex, toolEntry);\n}\n\nfunction processToolResultChunk(ws: WebSocket, clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[]): void {\n  for (const msg of clientMessages) {\n    log.debug('stream', 'forwarding client message to frontend', {\n      type: msg.type,\n      index: 'index' in msg ? msg.index : undefined,\n    });\n    sendJson(ws, msg);\n  }\n}\n\n// Retracts what the client saw: every assistant entry sent so far is re-sent blank.\nfunction processSuppressChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  // Closed as streamed so far: an open answer is then retracted like a sent one, and what follows opens new entries.\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n  log.debug('stream', 'suppression activated', {blankingEntries: state.sentAssistantIndices});\n\n  for (const idx of state.sentAssistantIndices) {\n    sendUpsert(ws, idx, {...session.entries[idx], content: ''} as SessionEntry);\n    markSuppressed(session, idx);\n  }\n\n  state.sentAssistantIndices = [];\n  state.suppressOutput = true;\n}\n\nfunction processStopChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n}\n\n// The parser holds back a tail that might open a tag; without this the tail of a cut-off answer would be lost.\nfunction flushHeldText(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  for (const segment of parser.flush()) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\n// The flag lives on the stored entry even when suppressed; only a visible entry is re-sent, which is how a live client learns it.\nfunction closeCutOff(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n  markTruncated(session, index);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, index, session.entries[index]);\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, key);\n}\n\nfunction commitOpenEntries(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n}\n\nfunction handlePing(ws: WebSocket, timestamp: number): void {\n  ws.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'pong', timestamp}));\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":29,"oldLines":7,"newStart":29,"newLines":10,"lines":[" "," type StateIndexKey = 'thinkingIndex' | '../../types/ai.js';\nimport {\n  createUserEntry,\n  createAssistantEntry,\n  createThinkingEntry,\n  createToolEntry,\n} from '../../orchestration/entryFactories.js';\nimport {appendEntry, appendDelta, commitEntry, markTruncated} from '../../orchestration/entryMutations.js';\nimport {sendJson, sendUpsert, sendDelta, sendCommit, sendSkip, sendError, sendStreamEnd} from './sender.js';\nimport {log} from '../../shared/logger.js';\n\ninterface StreamState {\n  thinkingIndex: number | null;\n  assistantIndex: number | null;\n  suppressOutput: boolean;\n  sentAssistantIndices: number[];\n}\n\ntype StateIndexKey = 'thinkingIndex' | 'assistantIndex';\ntype EntryFactory = typeof createThinkingEntry | typeof createAssistantEntry;\nexport function createMessageHandler(\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  sessionStore?: import('../types/sessionStore.js').SessionStore\n) {\n  // Keyed by session, not socket: a resumed session can be open on more than one (specs/hal-engine-architecture/spec.md).\n  const lock = new PerSessionLock();\n  return function handleMessage(\n    ws: WebSocket,\n    session: ChatSession,\n    rawMessage: unknown,\n    signal?: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const validation = validateMessage(rawMessage);\n    // Queued with no await before it, so a session's user_messages, a failed one too, are answered in arrival order.\n    if (isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage)) {\n      return lock.run(session.sessionId, () => answerInTurn(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n    }\n    // An async action runs up to its first await at once, so a ping is answered now and a throw stays a rejection.\n    return wsErrorHandler(ws, async () => answerFrame(ws, session, validation, sessionStore));\n  };\n}\n\n// The whole answer, an error and the stream_end included, goes out inside the lock: an error frame names no message.\nasync function answerInTurn(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  // Its socket closed while it waited: nobody is left to read the answer, and a session's last close took its credentials.\n  if (signal?.aborted || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSING || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSED) {\n    log.info('message', 'queued message dropped: its socket closed', {sessionId: session.sessionId});\n    return;\n  }\n  const startedAt = Date.now();\n  try {\n    await wsErrorHandler(ws, () => answerUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n  } finally {\n    endTurn(ws, session, signal, startedAt);\n  }\n}\n\n// A turn whose socket closed is abandoned rather than ended: nobody is left to read a terminal frame.\nfunction endTurn(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, signal: AbortSignal | undefined, startedAt: number): void {\n  if (signal?.aborted) {\n    log.info('message', 'turn abandoned: its socket closed', {\n      sessionId: session.sessionId,\n      elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n    });\n    return;\n  }\n  sendStreamEnd(ws);\n}\n\nasync function answerUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  const message = validation.valid ? validation.data : undefined;\n  if (message?.type === 'user_message') return handleUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, message.content, signal);\n  answerFrame(ws, validation);\n}\n\nfunction answerFrame(ws: WebSocket, validation: ValidationResult): void {\n  if (!validation.valid) {\n    log.warn('message', 'validation failed', {error: validation.error});\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.INVALID_MESSAGE, validation.error);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const message = validation.data;\n  if (message.type === 'ping') handlePing(ws, message.timestamp);\n}\n\n// Read from the raw frame, so a user_message that fails validation still gets its stream_end.\nfunction isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage: unknown): boolean {\n  return typeof rawMessage === 'object' && rawMessage !== null && Reflect.get(rawMessage, 'type') === 'user_message';\n}\n\nasync function wsErrorHandler(ws: WebSocket, action: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await action();\n  } catch (error) {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';\n    const code = error instanceof AIError ? error.code : 'UNKNOWN';\n    log.error('message', 'handling error', {error: message, code});\n    if (error instanceof AIError && error.code === 'RATE_LIMITED') {\n      sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.RATE_LIMITED, 'AI service is busy, please try again');\n      return;\n    }\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.SERVER_ERROR, 'Failed to process message');\n  }\n}\n\nasync function handleUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  content: string,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  log.info('message', 'user message received', {sessionId: session.sessionId, contentLength: content.length});\n\n  const userEntry = createUserEntry(content);\n  const userIndex = appendEntry(session, userEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, userIndex, userEntry);\n\n  const parser = new ThinkingTagParser();\n  const state: StreamState = {\n    thinkingIndex: null,\n    assistantIndex: null,\n    suppressOutput: false,\n    sentAssistantIndices: [],\n  };\n\n  let textChunkCount = 0;\n\n  try {\n    for await (const chunk of orchestrator.processMessageStream(session, {signal})) {\n      // Its socket closed: breaking returns the run's generator, so the run stops and its own teardown still runs.\n      if (signal?.aborted) break;\n      textChunkCount = logChunk(chunk, textChunkCount);\n      processChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk);\n    }\n  } finally {\n    // Anything still open here was cut short: a stop has already flushed the parser and committed every entry it opened.\n    flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n  }\n\n  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n  }\n  log.info('stream', 'stream completed', {suppressed: state.suppressOutput, entries: session.entries.length});\n}\n\n// Diagnostics only: counts a run of text chunks so it is logged once, not per token.\nfunction logChunk(chunk: MessageChunk, textChunkCount: number): number {\n  if (chunk.type === 'text') return textChunkCount + 1;\n\n  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n  }\n\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') {\n    log.debug('stream', 'tool_result chunk', {\n      messageCount: chunk.clientMessages.length,\n      types: chunk.clientMessages.map(m => m.type),\n    });\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  log.debug('stream', 'chunk', {type: chunk.type});\n  return 0;\n}\n\nfunction processChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n  chunk: MessageChunk\n): void {\n  if (chunk.type === 'text') return processTextChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk.text);\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_use') return processToolUseChunk(ws, session, chunk.toolCall);\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') return processToolResultChunk(ws, chunk.clientMessages);\n  if (chunk.type === 'suppress_output') return processSuppressChunk(ws, session, state);\n  if (chunk.type === 'stop') return processStopChunk(ws, session, state, parser);\n}\n\nfunction processTextChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n  text: string\n): void {\n  const segments = parser.push(text);\n  for (const segment of segments) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction handleTextSegment(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, segment: ParsedSegment): void {\n  if (segment.type === 'thinking') {\n    streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex', createThinkingEntry, segment.content);\n    return;\n  }\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex', createAssistantEntry, segment.content);\n}\n\nfunction streamSegment(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  key: StateIndexKey,\n  createEntry: EntryFactory,\n  content: string\n): void {\n  const isNew = initSegment(session, state, key, createEntry);\n  if (isNew && state.suppressOutput) skipSuppressed(ws, session, state[key]!);\n  if (isNew && !state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, state[key]!, session.entries[state[key]!]);\n  appendDelta(session, state[key]!, content);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendDelta(ws, state[key]!, content);\n}\n\n// Stored as suppressed, so a replay on resume shows the client what it saw live and not the text a tool hid.\nfunction skipSuppressed(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, index: number): void {\n  markSuppressed(session, index);\n  sendSkip(ws, index);\n}\n\nfunction markSuppressed(session: ChatSession, index: number): void {\n  const entry = session.entries[index];\n  if (entry.role === 'assistant' || entry.role === 'thinking') entry.suppressed = true;\n}\n\nfunction initSegment(session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey, createEntry: EntryFactory): boolean {\n  if (state[key] !== null) return false;\n  state[key] = appendEntry(session, createEntry());\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction commitAndClear(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n\n  commitEntry(session, index);\n  state[key] = null;\n\n  // Only what the client received can be retracted, and only assistant entries.\n  if (state.suppressOutput) return;\n\n  sendCommit(ws, index);\n  if (key === 'assistantIndex') state.sentAssistantIndices.push(index);\n}\n\nfunction processToolUseChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  toolCall: {name: string; input: Record<string, unknown>}\n): void {\n  const toolEntry = createToolEntry(toolCall.name, toolCall.input);\n  const toolIndex = appendEntry(session, toolEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, toolIndex, toolEntry);\n}\n\nfunction processToolResultChunk(ws: WebSocket, clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[]): void {\n  for (const msg of clientMessages) {\n    log.debug('stream', 'forwarding client message to frontend', {\n      type: msg.type,\n      index: 'index' in msg ? msg.index : undefined,\n    });\n    sendJson(ws, msg);\n  }\n}\n\n// Retracts what the client saw: every assistant entry sent so far is re-sent blank.\nfunction processSuppressChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  // Closed as streamed so far: an open answer is then retracted like a sent one, and what follows opens new entries.\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n  log.debug('stream', 'suppression activated', {blankingEntries: state.sentAssistantIndices});\n\n  for (const idx of state.sentAssistantIndices) {\n    sendUpsert(ws, idx, {...session.entries[idx], content: ''} as SessionEntry);\n    markSuppressed(session, idx);\n  }\n\n  state.sentAssistantIndices = [];\n  state.suppressOutput = true;\n}\n\nfunction processStopChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n}\n\n// The parser holds back a tail that might open a tag; without this the tail of a cut-off answer would be lost.\nfunction flushHeldText(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  for (const segment of parser.flush()) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\n// The flag lives on the stored entry even when suppressed; only a visible entry is re-sent, which is how a live client learns it.\nfunction closeCutOff(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n  markTruncated(session, index);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, index, session.entries[index]);\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, key);\n}\n\nfunction commitOpenEntries(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n}\n\nfunction handlePing(ws: WebSocket, timestamp: number): void {\n  ws.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'pong', timestamp}));\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":96,"oldLines":7,"newStart":96,"newLines":12,"lines":["   answerFrame(ws, validation);"," }"," ","-function answerFrame(ws: WebSocket, validation: {sendJson, sendUpsert, sendDelta, sendCommit, sendSkip, sendError, sendStreamEnd} from './sender.js';","newString":"import {sendJson, sendUpsert, sendDelta, sendCommit, sendSkip, sendError, sendStreamEnd, sendSessionList} from './sender.js';","originalFile":"import {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport type {SessionEntry} from '../../types/session.js';\nimport type {ChatSession} from '../../types/session.js';\nimport {ErrorCodes} from '../../types/messages.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../../types/messages.js';\nimport {ThinkingTagParser, ParsedSegment} from '../../infrastructure/parsers/thinkingTagParser.js';\nimport {validateMessage} from './validation.js';\nimport type {ValidationResult} from './validation.js';\nimport {PerSessionLock} from '../../shared/perSessionLock.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport {AIError} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport type {MessageChunk} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport {\n  createUserEntry,\n  createAssistantEntry,\n  createThinkingEntry,\n  createToolEntry,\n} from '../../orchestration/entryFactories.js';\nimport {appendEntry, appendDelta, commitEntry, markTruncated} from '../../orchestration/entryMutations.js';\nimport {sendJson, sendUpsert, sendDelta, sendCommit, sendSkip, sendError, sendStreamEnd} from './sender.js';\nimport {log} from '../../shared/logger.js';\n\ninterface StreamState {\n  thinkingIndex: number | null;\n  assistantIndex: number | null;\n  suppressOutput: boolean;\n  sentAssistantIndices: number[];\n}\n\ntype StateIndexKey = 'thinkingIndex' | 'assistantIndex';\ntype EntryFactory = typeof createThinkingEntry | typeof createAssistantEntry;\nexport function createMessageHandler(\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  sessionStore?: import('../types/sessionStore.js').SessionStore\n) {\n  // Keyed by session, not socket: a resumed session can be open on more than one (specs/hal-engine-architecture/spec.md).\n  const lock = new PerSessionLock();\n  return function handleMessage(\n    ws: WebSocket,\n    session: ChatSession,\n    rawMessage: unknown,\n    signal?: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const validation = validateMessage(rawMessage);\n    // Queued with no await before it, so a session's user_messages, a failed one too, are answered in arrival order.\n    if (isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage)) {\n      return lock.run(session.sessionId, () => answerInTurn(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n    }\n    // An async action runs up to its first await at once, so a ping is answered now and a throw stays a rejection.\n    return wsErrorHandler(ws, async () => answerFrame(ws, session, validation, sessionStore));\n  };\n}\n\n// The whole answer, an error and the stream_end included, goes out inside the lock: an error frame names no message.\nasync function answerInTurn(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  // Its socket closed while it waited: nobody is left to read the answer, and a session's last close took its credentials.\n  if (signal?.aborted || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSING || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSED) {\n    log.info('message', 'queued message dropped: its socket closed', {sessionId: session.sessionId});\n    return;\n  }\n  const startedAt = Date.now();\n  try {\n    await wsErrorHandler(ws, () => answerUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n  } finally {\n    endTurn(ws, session, signal, startedAt);\n  }\n}\n\n// A turn whose socket closed is abandoned rather than ended: nobody is left to read a terminal frame.\nfunction endTurn(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, signal: AbortSignal | undefined, startedAt: number): void {\n  if (signal?.aborted) {\n    log.info('message', 'turn abandoned: its socket closed', {\n      sessionId: session.sessionId,\n      elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n    });\n    return;\n  }\n  sendStreamEnd(ws);\n}\n\nasync function answerUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  const message = validation.valid ? validation.data : undefined;\n  if (message?.type === 'user_message') return handleUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, message.content, signal);\n  answerFrame(ws, validation);\n}\n\nasync function answerFrame(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  sessionStore?: import('../types/sessionStore.js').SessionStore\n): Promise<void> {\n  if (!validation.valid) {\n    log.warn('message', 'validation failed', {error: validation.error});\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.INVALID_MESSAGE, validation.error);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const message = validation.data;\n  if (message.type === 'ping') handlePing(ws, message.timestamp);\n  if (message.type === 'list_sessions') await handleListSessions(ws, session, message, ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey, createEntry: EntryFactory): boolean {\n  if (state[key] !== null) return false;\n  state[key] = appendEntry(session, createEntry());\n  return commitEntry(session, index);\n  state[key] = null;\n\n  // Only what the client received can be retracted, and only assistant entries.\n  if (state.suppressOutput) return;\n\n  sendCommit(ws, Record<string, unknown>}\n): void {\n  const toolEntry = createToolEntry(toolCall.name, toolCall.input);\n  const toolIndex = appendEntry(session, toolEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, toolIndex, toolEntry);\n}\n\nfunction processToolResultChunk(ws: WebSocket, clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[]): void {\n  for (const msg of clientMessages) {\n    log.debug('stream', 'forwarding client message to frontend', {\n      type: msg.type,\n      index: 'index' in msg ? msg.index : undefined,\n    });\n    sendJson(ws, msg);\n  }\n}\n\n// Retracts what the client saw: every assistant entry sent so far is re-sent blank.\nfunction processSuppressChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  // Closed as streamed so far: an open answer is then retracted like a sent one, and what follows opens new entries.\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n  log.debug('stream', 'suppression activated', {blankingEntries: state.sentAssistantIndices});\n\n  for (const idx of state.sentAssistantIndices) {\n    sendUpsert(ws, idx, {...session.entries[idx], content: ''} as SessionEntry);\n    markSuppressed(session, idx);\n  }\n\n  state.sentAssistantIndices = [];\n  state.suppressOutput = true;\n}\n\nfunction processStopChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n}\n\n// The parser holds back a tail that might open a tag; without this the tail of a cut-off answer would be lost.\nfunction flushHeldText(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  for (const segment of parser.flush()) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\n// The flag lives on the stored entry even when suppressed; only a visible entry is re-sent, which is how a live client learns it.\nfunction closeCutOff(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n  markTruncated(session, index);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, index, session.entries[index]);\n
